Dates 2020-01-02..2024-12-20 only (no ≥2025). Manager pass bar: 30bp, matched win rate >50%, matched residual CI clears 0 in tagged direction.

- Tested cells: 666 / 720.
- BH survivors (30bp, any H): 236.
